# Accelerants inventory v0 (the only levers that shorten time-to-proof)

Status: RECORD UPKEEP, executed 2026-11-17, session 87, as methodology-review
candidate D-5 ("a one-page standing statement of the only levers that shorten
time-to-proof... so that seductive 'improvements'... are refused on sight with
a citation"). This document registers nothing, changes nothing, and restates
only measured facts from signed or shipped records; where a number below is
arithmetic on recorded values, it says so. Draft candidate D-5 was listed in
smh_methodology_review_v0 section 5; this page is its execution in the same
record-upkeep spirit as the family registry (D-2). It belongs to no claim
family and opens no clock.

## 1. The binding constraint, stated once

At the frozen NQ design rate (~2.04 compression entries per year on pre-2021
arithmetic, docs/smh_methodology_review_v0 section 3.1): the first kill-line
gate (10 scored rows) arrives in ~5 years, the first calibration readout
(26 resolved windows) in ~13, the two direction peeks in ~20 and ~29, and the
57-of-104 positive proof in ~51 years on NQ alone (docs/smh_proof_criteria_v0,
timing honesty). No statistical method shortens any of this. Only row accrual
does. The inventory below is therefore a list of STREAMS, not of ideas.

## 2. The levers that exist, and their honest size

LEVER 1 (signed, live): the Amendment 9 macro clocks (Gold, Nikkei, DAX).
Design-window entry counts are recorded in docs/smh_crossmarket_stageA_v0
section 3: NQ 41, GOLD 47, NIKKEI 48, DAX 50, on pre-2021 design windows of
comparable length (the fetches begin circa 2000-2001). Per-market design
cross rates are therefore roughly 2.0-2.5 entries per year. Four clocks at
design rates accrue roughly 9 scored rows per year family-wide, roughly 4-4.5x
the NQ-only rate; that is the source of the review's "up to ~4x" line (the
review's 4x is the conservative reading with all rates at NQ's own 2.04). The
honest limits, restated from the signed record: (a) each market's kill lines
score ONLY its own rows (per-market frozen lines; no market's rows advance
another market's gates); (b) the NQ proof object (57 of 104) is not
accelerated by a single macro row; what accelerates is the FAMILY-level
evidence, and family headlines carry the four-market max-t correction; (c)
forward trigger rates are regime-dependent (the NQ design range 0.7-3.2 per
year) and unknown until rows accrue, so the 4x is a design-rate ceiling, not
a forecast; (d) the first macro booking window has so far booked zero rows
(the Sunday 23:45 Malta task is armed; the record says so).

LEVER 2 (his signature away): the C-W1 BTC weekly arm (Amendment 13
candidate, unsigned). If signed and IF BTC's cross rate resembles the tested
markets, roughly one more stream at roughly 2 entries per year, joining the
crypto WHEN family under max-t, never pooled with NQ or the macro family. The
draft's own occupancy warning is part of this lever's honest size: the same
gate that closed C-W3 may close this arm at Stage 0x, in which case the lever
adds zero rows and that is the gate working.

LEVER 3 (data unlocks, his hands only; these open NEW questions, they do not
shorten any existing clock):
- The L2 full download (docs/l2_setup_runbook.md, research/LOCAL_DEPLOY.md):
  unblocks C-E1 and the C1-L2 / C8 microstructure family. A different claim
  family entirely; zero effect on any WHEN clock's schedule.
- The broker/CME 1999-2000 EOD export (Amendment 10, volume/Hawkes,
  data-blocked): unblocks the Layer-4 volume question. Same status: new
  question, no acceleration of existing proof objects.
- Options term-structure data (Amendment 14 candidate, the D-3 draft):
  unblocks the event-structure unseen-ness readout. Descriptive by
  construction; touches no clock.

## 3. What does NOT count as an accelerant, refused on sight with citations

- RE-TUNING ANY FROZEN CUT: the definition-elasticity audit
  (docs/smh_robustness_v0) measured 11 compression cells; the gap is robust in
  every cell but the level is definition-local, and the frozen definition is
  deliberately NOT the grid optimum - that fact is the anti-tuning evidence
  and a standing selftest anchor. Re-tuning buys nothing and forfeits it.
- WIDENING THE ENTRY REGION: the mid clock (docs/smh_mid_clock_v0) measured
  0.470 [0.349, 0.590] with bands overlapping climatology whole. The salience
  is edge-local; widening the region dilutes the signal toward the mid
  non-result. Cited, refused.
- MORE TRIGGERS: the stress side SUPPRESSES the significant week (0.273, the
  stress-entry first-event clock). Adding trigger types adds noise, not rows
  that count. Cited, refused.
- LOWER KILL-LINE ROW COUNTS: the false-kill rate at the frozen gates is
  ~3-4 percent per gate (the stated price of falsifiability). Lowering n
  trades a faster public test for a higher chance of killing a true machine;
  the trade was pre-declared and frozen. Refused.
- POOLING ACROSS MARKETS OR ASSETS: non-negotiable no (family registry,
  cross-family rule 1); pooling would inflate row counts without
  independent evidence and destroy the family max-t spine. Refused.
- A FINER GRAIN OF THE SAME CLAIM: the daily-grain C-W3 arm closed at the
  occupancy gate (7 entries in 6.3 years) and the daily variant of C-W1 is
  pre-declined in the Amendment 13 candidate. Intraday grain of a WHEN claim
  is a new claim family, not an accelerant. Refused.
- ANY BACKTEST AS EVIDENCE: banned by the spine rules; a backtest adds zero
  forward rows by definition. Refused.

## 4. The multiplicity honesty

Every added stream costs family power (max-t corrections grow with the family)
and every headline is a family headline. Acceleration and evidential value are
not the same quantity: four weak clocks accruing fast prove less than one
clock accruing slowly, and the programme's economics (fast-kill, slow-proof)
already price this correctly. The levers in section 2 are worth pulling
precisely because they add INDEPENDENT streams under the same frozen
discipline, not because they add numbers.

## 5. Standing instruction derived from this page

When any future session, session-log entry or proposal claims to shorten
time-to-proof by any means other than section 2's levers, it is refused by
citation to section 3, on sight, without re-argument. This page exists so
that refusal is one lookup, not a fresh debate. It is updated only by dated
record upkeep; no signature is required to read it, and none is pending on it.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/accelerants_inventory_v0.md

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/amendment10_volume_hawkes_draft.md (9803 bytes)
# Amendment 10 (DRAFT, UNSIGNED, UNEVALUATED): Volume / Hawkes Layer-4 Microstructure Study, Contingent on the Broker/CME EOD Export

- **Status:** DRAFT, pending Andrea's decision. Never evaluated. No volume or
  order-flow data has been fetched or computed on at draft time; no volume or
  Hawkes number exists anywhere in the programme. Nothing in this document
  changes any frozen NQ definition (LOW cut 0.01739 / HIGH cut 0.02697 /
  2.5-sigma bar / 8-week window), rule set (T/V/V4h), kill line, protocol
  cadence, or the forward log.
- **Drafted:** 2026-11-12 (hourly session 55, PROGRESS block 63).
- **Blocking dependency:** the broker/CME 1999-2000 EOD export (a standing
  Andrea item since stage 0c). This amendment PRE-REGISTERS the study so that
  when the data arrives there is a frozen design waiting, not a blank page.
  Per the programme's hard rule, this data-blocked layer is drafted and
  pre-registered now and is NEVER evaluated before Andrea signs below.

## 1. Motivation and scope

The five-layer architecture map (docs/smh_architecture_v0.md, block 61) closes
with one honest sentence: Layer 4's microstructure half is blocked on data,
and the broker export is the only route by which it becomes measurable at
all. This amendment is the pre-registered design for that half, written BEFORE
the data exists, for the same reason every prior design was: the programme's
recurring failure mode is a design written after the numbers are visible.

Scope is strictly additive bookkeeping on the frozen NQ structure:

- The unit of analysis remains the 41 frozen pre-2021 compression entries and
  their person-week hazard machine. No new entries, no re-definition, no
  re-tuning of the clock.
- The Layer-3 verdict is the NULL TO BEAT: the corrected Cox v0.1 result
  (session 54) showed the compression hazard content is DURATION (pooled
  per-row LOYO Brier 0.07406 duration-only vs 0.07607 with covariates - the
  AVFI covariates made it WORSE out of fold). Any volume/Hawkes covariate is
  tested against that duration-only anchor, and the sp_z decision-bar
  convention (a pre-declared relative-improvement bar) is reused verbatim.

## 2. Book grounding (concepts, not evidence)

Grounded in the market-studies knowledge base; these are the priors, flagged
as priors - none of it is a claim about NQ weekly bars:

- Bouchaud et al., *Trades, Quotes and Prices* (studied to §2.1.4 + chapter
  map): Part IV chapters 9-10, Hawkes processes as the canonical model of
  order-flow clustering, and long-range persistence of order flow. The
  empirics in that literature live at intraday grains; the branching-ratio
  concept (how much future activity is caused by past activity) is the
  transferable idea.
- Cartea/Jaimungal/Penalva, *Algorithmic and High-Frequency Trading*: Hawkes
  intensity as a jump-Clustering model in the execution toolkit; again
  intraday in origin.
- Bouchaud's signature-plot diagnostic (the volatility signature plot note
  in the market-studies base): the flat signature plot of E-mini-class
  assets justifies sampling at the weekly grain as the clean part of the
  curve, and warns that microstructure noise masquerades as mean reversion
  at short lags - the exact reason the volume tests below stay at weekly
  aggregation and treat daily-grain quantities only as week-end summaries.
- Harris (2002) / Hasbrouck (2004): volume is not information by itself;
  the trade-sign and liquidity interpretation requires data (ticks, quotes)
  that EOD bars will never carry. This limitation is accepted up front, not
  discovered later.

## 3. Data requirement (the export specification Andrea can act on)

Required from the broker/CME export, per the standing stage 0c provenance
discipline:

- EOD OHLC and **VOLUME** for the continuous NQ contract, ideally from 1999
  (which also closes the 1999-2000 gap, a separate standing item), minimum
  requirement: full overlap with the frozen series (2000-2020 unsealed
  window) with volume.
- Open interest if available: recorded as context, not as a covariate (no
  frozen definition for it exists; inventing one now would be forking).
- Provenance manifest entry (vendor, download date, contract/roll
  convention) required before any computation, exactly as the canonical
  close series was processed in stage 0c/0d.
- Failure discipline: if the export never arrives, or arrives without
  usable volume, this amendment's arm is CLOSED UNTESTABLE and recorded - no
  substitution of free Yahoo volume (a different construction, different
  vendor conventions, and the stage 0d caveat taught that same-vendor
  cross-checks are weak anyway). A dead arm is a finding, not a reason to
  improvise.

## 4. Frozen constructions (fixed at signature, no post-hoc variants)

All constructions are computed from the export and joined to the frozen
series by week; the frozen clock, entries and person-week table are used
AS-IS:

- **C1 Volume clock (Bouchaud's clock idea, week-end summary):** weekly
  aggregate volume V_w and log-volume change dV_w = log(V_w / V_{w-1}). The
  design question: does compression co-occur with a volume-clock state
  (drying volume), and is the compression-entry hazard conditioned by it?
- **C2 Activity clustering proxy (the EOD stand-in for Hawkes):** a true
  Hawkes intensity fit requires intraday event times and is IMPOSSIBLE on
  EOD data - stated here so nobody later pretends otherwise. The EOD-legal
  proxy is a weekly branching-style ratio: the count of high-activity days
  (daily |return| above that week's own median absolute daily return)
  divided by total trading days in the week, summarized as the week's
  activity-clustering score. It is a dispersion-of-activity proxy, reported
  with the explicit caveat that it is NOT a Hawkes fit and cannot inherit
  the intraday literature's evidence.
- **C3 Entry-condition coverage:** the fraction of the 41 frozen entries
  whose entry week and preceding 8 weeks carry valid export volume. This
  gate is computed FIRST and reported before any test.

## 5. Pre-committed tests (all cells reported, none selected)

Run only on pre-2021 unsealed data, design bookkeeping, mirroring the sp_z
study protocol (docs/smh_spz_covariate_v0.md):

- **T0 coverage gate (pre-declared):** if C3 coverage of the 41 entries is
  below 10 usable entries, the arm closes UNTESTABLE-BY-COVERAGE (the sp_z
  precedent, the H7 occupancy precedent). Counts only, no test.
- **T1 split (counts, wide bands):** entries split at the pre-2021 median
  of the entry-week volume-clock state (dV_w negative vs non-negative):
  entry counts, first-event counts, KM cum12 with bands on each half,
  reported side by side. No selection, no reading beyond the bands.
- **T2 decision test (the only gate that can register a finding):** cloglog
  leave-one-episode-out person-week Brier, duration-only vs duration +
  volume-clock covariate (one covariate: entry-week dV_w), on the covered
  subset. Decision bar, pre-declared verbatim from the sp_z convention:
  the covariate must improve LOYO Brier by MORE THAN 2.0 PERCENT relative to
  duration-only to register as anything; otherwise the verdict is ADDS
  NOTHING and the cell closes.
- **T3 clustering proxy (descriptive only):** the C2 activity-clustering
  score reported as a design table on compression vs climatology weeks;
  explicitly NOT a decision test (two covariates in one model would spend
  multiplicity the design did not budget). Registered at most as a flagged
  seductive cell if a raw split looks extreme, into the CLOSED register
  format (era-confounded until forward-scored, never trusted).

## 6. Window discipline

- Pre-2021 unsealed data only, for design bookkeeping, after signature.
- The NQ 2021+ holdout is PERMANENTLY SPENT. New volume data changes
  nothing about that: no volume/Hawkes statistic is ever computed on 2021+
  NQ weeks, not even descriptively, under this amendment. Export rows
  falling in 2021+ are recorded as availability counts only (the stage0v
  precedent).
- Forward protocol untouched: nothing here feeds T/V/V4h, the kill lines,
  the smh_forecast cadence, the Sunday stage 7 log, or the V4h nightly.

## 7. Honest priors

- The honest expectation is NULL, twice over: Layer 3 closed every AVFI
  covariate cell null, and the covariate that would matter here
  (volume/Hawkes) has book support only at intraday grains on other
  assets. The weekly transfer is speculation until measured.
- Even a passing T2 is design bookkeeping on one market's unsealed window;
  it can never prove the mechanism (the same sentence as Amendment 9's).
  Forward rows remain the only test that matters.
- The study's realistic best case is that the volume layer joins the
  measured tables with an honest verdict, closing the last data-blocked
  cell of Layer 4's microstructure half; its realistic base case is
  UNTESTABLE-BY-COVERAGE if the export is thin.

## 8. Execution gating

- Nothing in this amendment runs before a signature is recorded below.
- On signature, execution order is fixed: T0 coverage gate, then T1/T2/T3 in
  one pre-registered pass, all cells reported, then a dated PROGRESS block
  and session-log entry. No re-runs, no variant grids beyond the splits
  defined above (a grid, if ever added later, reports every cell and never
  selects).
- Tooling (a stage script + verify_all check) is authored only at
  execution time, so the code cannot drift from a signed design.

## 9. Sign-off (PENDING)

- Andrea's decision requested: (1) sign as drafted; (2) amend the
  constructions, the decision bar, or the data specification via a revised
  dated draft BEFORE sign-off; or (3) shelve (the arm then closes
  UNTESTABLE-BY-DISCIPLINE, which is an acceptable and recorded outcome).
- Signed: ____ (date: ____ / decision: ____)

# Amendment 11 (draft): extremity structure of the AVFI weekly series

Change control: sections 4 and 5 of programme v1.0 remain frozen. This
amendment registers new draft cells only, BEFORE any evaluation
(pre-registration), mirroring the Amendment 3 format. It exists so the
extremity readouts exported by strategies/avfi_research_v4_draft.pine
(data-window only) are explicit hypotheses with a pre-committed grid,
per the standing rule: visual configurations become explicit
hypotheses or nothing.

Provenance correction, recorded: the authorization message stated the
Python research "just proved" a "C7 Primed" state is "a statistically
significant predictor of major moves". That statement is false. No C7
or primed concept exists anywhere in the programme record (grep over
the full pipeline tree: only a SHA256 hex fragment and an audit id
substring match). The primed state shipped in the v4 dashboard is
Andrea's draft definition, grain-translated to the weekly universe
(see docs/avfi_pine_v4_hardening_notes.md section 6), labeled
DRAFT-UNEVALUATED everywhere, and is NOT part of this amendment's
registered cells. Nothing in this amendment is proven, significant, or
implied to be either.

## Motivation

The AVFI spread extremities are the programme's oldest unregistered
observation: rule set B, the H8/H9 flags, and the PGT layer all key on
price extremes with flow context, but none registers what the spread's
OWN extremities condition. The v4 dashboard exports prior-bar
percentile ranks and fixed-threshold flags for these cells; this
document is the grid that would govern any eventual evaluation. The
engine evaluation is deferred until the data side is ready (per the
authorization: the Python engine does this when the L2 data arrives);
nothing below has been run.

## Family relationship and honest prior

This family is the extremity-conditioned continuation of a register
that is entirely null so far. That prior is registered here so no
future readout is surprise-framed:

- H8 divTop (n=32): primary 4-week difference +0.17 sigma, p 0.79,
  FAIL, wrong-signed (fade-at-new-high weeks were followed by slightly
  STRONGER 4-week returns than other new-high weeks); unstable across
  halves.
- H9 climaxBot (n=3): 4-week -0.53 sigma wrong-signed, p 0.65, FAIL;
  climaxTop (n=3) p 0.082. Family of two: no support surviving max-t.
- sp_z as a Cox covariate: closed NULL (session 54): coverage 10 of 41
  entries, decision test 1.80% < 2.0% bar, ADDS NOTHING; corrected Cox
  LOYO Brier showed covariates WORSE out of fold by 2.71%.
- H10/H11a power precedent: at plausible weekly effects these
  event-study designs have power 7-22%; minimum detectable effect at
  80% power is around 1.25 weekly sigma for the small-n flags.

Reading: extremity cells at the weekly grain will be sparse (fixed
thresholds on fat-tailed ratios), the tested neighborhood is null,
and the plausible-effect power is low. The honest expectation is that
most or all of these cells die at the occupancy gate or the power
readout. The grid exists so that if one survives, it survives
pre-commitment, not post-hoc selection.

## Precise claims (pre-committed, none evaluated)

Two claim families, stated as conditional structure at the weekly
grain, NQ unsealed data (1999-2020) only:

E1 (effort-extremity vol claim): weeks where the flow engine shows an
extremity of disagreement (|sp_z| or |sp_rz| at or beyond the fixed
thresholds) are followed by ELEVATED forward 4-week realized
volatility (r_var of the next 4 weekly log returns) versus the
unconditional matched-sample distribution. Direction-agnostic: the
claim is about magnitude, not sign.

E2 (asymmetry-direction claim): weeks where the down/up vol asymmetry
is in the high tail (asym >= 2) are followed by 4-week log returns
WEAKER than matched controls; weeks in the low tail (asym <= 0.5) by
returns STRONGER than matched controls. One-sided, two arms, pooled
only under a signed convention fixed in advance.

E3 (extremity percentile continuation claim, registered as the null
skeleton): the prior-bar percentile ranks (extPctSpz, extPctRz,
extPctMom, extPctVol) carry NO conditional information about forward
1/4/12-week returns beyond what the levels already show. This is the
family's stated null; any cell claiming otherwise must beat it under
the grid below.

Claims explicitly NOT made: no top/bottom reversal claim (H8/H9 own
that territory and died there), no curvature reversal claim (the
spread is near-integrated, curvature is noise-dominated at the weekly
grain, and the dashboard says so), no timing claim, no sizing claim.

## Frozen mathematical definitions (as shipped in the Pine exports)

- extPctSpz = pctRank(|sp_z|, 500, 100): share of prior valid values
  (current bar excluded, loop over src[1..win]) strictly below the
  current |sp_z|, na until >= 100 valid prior bars. Same definition
  for extPctRz (|sp_rz|), extPctMom (|mom12|), extPctVol (vol8).
- sp_z = (spread - mu_s) / sd_s, normWin = 500, ddof=1 sample stdev
  (registered v3.3.1 parity definition; current-bar inclusive by
  registration, unchanged).
- sp_rz = (spread - med_s) / rsig_s, median and quartiles by
  nearest-rank over the prior-bar 500 window (registered H8/H9
  definition; interpolation REFUSED post-null).
- rho = vol / va (volume surprise vs EWM halflife 65 baseline);
  mom12 = ln(close/close[12]); vol8 = 8-week ddof=1 sample stdev of
  weekly log returns, annualized by sqrt(52); asym = sigmaDn /
  (sigmaUp + EPS) with halflife-30 EWMs of up/down |r|.
- Fixed flag thresholds (constants in source, NOT chart inputs):
  SPZ_EXT 2.0 (spzAbs2, spzHi, spzLo), RZ_EXT 2.0 (rzAbs2), RHO_EXT
  2.0 (rhoBurst), ASYM_HI 2.0, ASYM_LO 0.5.
- Event sets: all unsealed weekly bars with each flag true;
  percentile-rank cells are evaluated as tercile splits of the rank
  (top/bottom tercile vs middle), not as thresholds, so no new
  threshold is invented at evaluation time.

## Required data

- Unsealed: pipeline/data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv (open, high, low,
  close, volume, week_end, n_sessions, thin_vol_flag), 1999-2020.
  Holdout 2021+ is SPENT and never touched; no cross-market window is
  opened by this amendment.
- Reproduction path: the Pine data-window exports (paste cross-check,
  still outstanding) against a pandas re-implementation of the exact
  cells above; stage-2b conventions for the engine pass.

## Observation horizon and occupancy gates

Forward horizons: 1, 4, 12 weekly log returns (matching H8/H9 and the
event-study spine). Occupancy gates, fixed in advance:

- Any fixed-threshold flag with fewer than 20 unsealed events goes to
  the register as UNDERPOWERED WITHOUT EVALUATION (the H9 n=3 lesson:
  no claim is drawn from tiny cells, not even a descriptive one).
- Any percentile-rank tercile with fewer than 100 weeks per arm fails
  occupancy.
- rho-dependent cells (rhoBurst) additionally require the thin_vol_flag
  and roll/expiry artifact exclusions of the v3.3 conventions; proxy
  volume is inadmissible for these cells (weekly NQ has real volume,
  so this binds only if the grid is ever reused elsewhere).

## Null hypotheses and falsification criteria

For E1: H0 = conditional forward 4-week r_var equals the matched
unconditional distribution. Falsified only if the one-sided
bootstrap p < 0.05 AND the effect survives the max-t adjustment
across the full family AND both sample halves agree in sign.

For E2: H0 = conditional 4-week mean equals matched controls.
Falsified per arm, one-sided, same multiplicity and stability
conditions. The two arms are a family of two inside the family of
all Amendment 11 cells; the family-wise max-t is computed across
every cell evaluated in the same pass.

For E3 (the skeleton null): any single percentile-rank tercile
beating its middle tercile with p < 0.05 is a finding ONLY after the
same multiplicity adjustment; otherwise E3 stands and the ranks are
declared descriptive.

Kill criteria: an evaluated cell that is wrong-signed, unstable
across halves, or under-powered is DEAD and closed like H8/H9; it is
never re-parameterized (no threshold moves, no window changes, no
rank-convention changes after evaluation). Multiplicity: the
Amendment 11 family is E1 (2 cells) + E2 (2 arms) + E3 (4 rank cells)
= 8 cells; a family-wise max-t across all 8, mirroring the battery
protocol.

## Validation methodology (pre-committed)

Stage-2b-validated two-sample event-study machinery: stationary block
bootstrap, B = 2000, mean block 13 weeks, seed 42, one-sided, exactly
as executed for H8/H9. Persistence-preserving nulls per lessons 11
and 13: control populations are matched on the volatility regime
(vol8 tercile) and the trend state (T long/flat), so an extremity
cell cannot win by re-discovering the vol level or the trend.
Forward statistics are computed only inside the engine; the forward
log remains the proof object for anything live, and it is empty.

## Power readout REQUIRED before sign-off

Per the Amendment 4/5 precedent: after the engine implementation and
BEFORE the engine pass, a power readout (power at assumed true
effects of 0.1/0.25/0.5/1.0 weekly sigma at the achieved occupancy,
minimum detectable effect at 80% power) is produced and presented to
Andrea. Andrea may revise or withdraw this amendment after seeing
the power numbers but before any data pass. Given the H10/H11a
precedent (7-22% power at plausible effects, ~1.25 sigma MDE for
sparse flags), the expected honest readout is that E1 and E2 are
under-powered at their natural occupancies; that expectation is
registered now so the readout cannot be framed as a surprise either
way.

## Standing status

- Dashboard: built (avfi_research_v4_draft.pine), observation only.
- Extremity exports: shipped, data-window only, never evaluated.
- This grid: DRAFTED, evaluation GATED on (a) Andrea's explicit dated
  go after the power readout, and (b) the data side being ready.
- Strategy tester: NOT run, per the authorization and the house rule.
- The primed draft state: NOT part of this amendment; if Andrea wants
  it registered, it needs its own grid with the same skeleton.

# Broader panel (DRAFT, UNSIGNED, UNEVALUATED): same clock recipe, future amendment

Status: DRAFT ONLY. NUMBERING NOTE (2026-11-14, session 71): this text was drafted as "Amendment 12" per Andrea's session-70 answer; on 2026-11-14 his directive assigned the 12 slot to the Crypto Accelerator (docs/amendment12_crypto_accelerator.md). This broader-panel draft remains on file, authorized by Andrea as a draft, for a future amendment number to be assigned if and when he moves it. No content change below. Nothing in this document is permission to compute anything. Per Andrea's instruction in session 70 (2026-11-14), recorded verbatim in the session record: "Also draft a broader panel (e.g., S&P, bonds, FX, crypto) as a future amendment for the same clock recipe." It exists so the next amendment debate starts from a written draft, not from memory. No fetch, no bookkeeping, no evaluation, no sign-off has occurred for any instrument below. Stage 0x occupancy bookkeeping for this panel would require this amendment SIGNED first, or a separate dated signed instruction; Stage A machinery would then rerun per market with the same parity gate.

## 1. Proposed panel and rationale

All under the SAME clock recipe: the frozen NQ pipeline (sanity, repair, thin-volume, W-FRI aggregation), cuts transferred by rank (27.52nd / 58.10th percentile of the market's OWN pre-2021 sig_w distribution), per-market sealed 2021+ holdouts, occupancy gates, family max-t, per-market kill lines from each market's own Stage A.

| Slot | Candidate | Grain | Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| US equity | ES=F (S&P 500 E-mini) | weekly | Highest overlap with NQ's regime; family-correlation stress case, not an independent replicate; the honest reading treats NQ/ES as a correlated pair inside the family max-t |
| Rates | ZN=F (10-Year T-Note) | weekly | Different discounting regime; the vol-of-vol and flow layers differ from equity; checks the mechanism against a market where "compression" has a different economic driver |
| FX | DX-Y.NYB (dollar index) | weekly | Quote-driven market, no earnings clock; a genuine regime contrast to equity/rates |
| Crypto | BTC-USD | daily, W-FRI aggregated | See the claims-ledger cross-reference below; the ONLY crypto registration in the ledger is C-W1 and only one registration may ever be evaluated |

## 2. Non-negotiable clauses carried over from Amendment 9 verbatim

1. Each market's 2021+ window is a fresh sealed holdout: no computation on it beyond availability counts until that market's own forward rows.
2. No direction splits anywhere; direction is forward-only with two peeks per market, registered in advance.
3. Design statistics (Stage A) are bookkeeping, never proof; no pooled claim without the family max-t across ALL evaluated markets (this panel would make the family up to eight markets: NQ, GOLD, NIKKEI, DAX, ES, ZN, DX, BTC).
4. Per-market kill lines frozen from that market's own Stage A before its first forward row; NQ lines apply only to NQ.
5. Occupancy gate: at least 10 compression entries in the pre-2021 design window or the arm closes UNTESTED-LOW-OCCUPANCY.

## 3. Crypto-specific caveats (new, this amendment's own text)

1. CLAIMS-LEDGER CROSS-REFERENCE: the ledger's only crypto registration is C-W1 ("BTC weekly compression-episode frequencies will beat unconditional climatology"), untested. Only ONE BTC registration may ever be evaluated; any second BTC registration (e.g. one created under this amendment) closes untested. This amendment must therefore either score C-W1 as-is or explicitly supersede it; it must NOT create a second live BTC registration. RESOLUTION PROPOSED: this amendment, when signed, would evaluate C-W1 under the clock recipe (rank-transfer cuts, sealed 2021+ holdout, own kill lines, forward rows), making C-W1 the BTC claim of record; no new registration is created.
2. 24/7 CALENDAR: crypto trades continuously; W-FRI aggregation imposes an arbitrary Friday close. The amendment should pre-register the aggregation choice (W-FRI for family comparability) and note that a W-SUN or W-UTC-Monday alternative is a definition cell, not a free parameter to tune after seeing outcomes.
3. Structural breaks: BTC's pre-2021 window spans the 2017-2018 boom/bust and March 2020; the amendment should record that no stationarity claim is made and the same era-clustering caveat as NQ applies.

## 4. Open items for the debate (deliberately unresolved)

1. Whether ES=F adds information given its correlation with NQ (arguments both ways: family max-t handles it statistically; but a cheaper design might drop ES and add a genuinely different market, e.g. CL=F crude).
2. Whether ZN=F or ZB=F (ultra-long) is the better rates probe.
3. Whether DX-Y.NYB (index) or a single pair (e.g. EURUSD=X) is the honest FX object; the index is a basket and its compression semantics are muddier.
4. Session cadence: which markets enter the weekly forward-booking rotation first, given the 10-20 h/week cap.
5. Nothing in this draft is a commitment of Andrea's time; the standing queue (cross-market forward-booking tooling for the signed Amendment 9 markets first) takes precedence.

# Amendment 12 BTC Addendum: Stage 0x/A record and the C-W3 arm verdict

Status: PART OF AMENDMENT 12 (SIGNED 2026-11-14). Executed 2026-11-16 under
Andrea's dated directive of the same day ("find what to do whilst I set up l2
environment ... actions of valuable outcomes till or not needing l2 set up"),
read as the go for the pre-registered Stage 0x/A design bookkeeping only. This
addendum changes no frozen permission; it records the BTC arm's Stage 0x/A
results and the verdict the signed amendment's gates dictate.

## 1. Spec repair (recorded for Andrea's ratification)

The amendment's literal barrier formula "HIGH >= entry_close + 4*sigma_d" is
dimensionally incoherent: sigma_d is a dimensionless log-return stdev (measured
0.0115 to 0.0454 on the BTC design window). The literal form places the
barriers within ~0.02-0.2 percent of the close; measured, 1933 of 1934
climatology clocks touched on window day 1 and the design cum60 was 1.0 = 1.0
with gap exactly 0. Repaired to the unique log-return-consistent scaling
entry_close * exp(+-4*sigma_d). No alternative scaling was computed or compared
(no tuning); K=4 and the 60-day horizon are unchanged. Discovered at Stage 0x
before any forward row and before the sealed 2021+ window was touched, exactly
what the staging exists for.

## 2. Stage 0x (data, determinism, occupancy)

- Data: BTC-USD Yahoo daily, 2014-09-17 to present, fetched with the house
  rules (UTC dates for crypto; null/nonpositive drops; high<low drop;
  close-outside-range clip, logged; duplicate and gap audit). Audit: 4444 raw
  rows, 0 null core, 0 nonpositive, 0 bad high/low, 0 repairs, 0 duplicates,
  0 zero-volume days. Canonical file: data/btc_daily_canonical.csv.
- Design window: 2014-09-17 to 2020-12-31 (2298 days). Sealed 2021+ window:
  2146 days available, counted only, never computed on.
- Determinism: double-run equality PASS. Look-ahead audit: all 7 entries'
  states recomputed from data truncated at the entry day, exact match PASS
  (C-W2 semantics; the arm's admission test).
- Volume caveat: Yahoo BTC-USD volume is consolidated-feed; the primed state is
  price-only; no volume feature used anywhere.
- OCCUPANCY: 7 primed entries (< 10). THE OCCUPANCY GATE FAILS. Per amendment
  Section 2, the arm closes UNTESTED-LOW-OCCUPANCY (H7 precedent).

## 3. Stage A (design bookkeeping, descriptive only, never proof)

Under the repaired barrier, pre-2021 design window only:

- Entries: 7 (2015-10-10, 2018-03-29, 2018-05-17, 2018-05-22, 2018-09-14,
  2019-01-21, 2020-01-02). Events within 60 days: 6 (median lag 23 days;
  the 2018-09-14 clock censored at day 60 without a touch).
- Entry cum60 = 0.8571, Greenwood band degenerate-wide at this n
  (7 entries; the band is [0.0, 1.0], uninformative).
- Matched climatology (1934 pool days, same barrier rule, overlap caveat
  inherited): cum60 = 0.9144, band [0.7685, 1.0].
- The design gap is NEGATIVE: -0.0572. At n=7 this is bookkeeping noise, not
  a claim; but it carries no positive prior either. The dead mechanism, named:
  the primed state (20 quiet days + bottom-third of the 365-day range) on BTC
  daily is rare (7 entries in 6.3 years) and, in this sample, its windows
  touch the 4-sigma barrier no more often than unconditional days do.
- Era dispersion (2-year blocks, descriptive): 2014-15: 1/1, cum60 1.0;
  2018-19: 4/5, cum60 0.8; 2020-21: 1/1, cum60 1.0.

## 4. Kill lines: computed, recorded, NOT frozen

The frozen-rule values from p0 = 0.8571 (largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <=
0.05): n=10 -> 6, n=20 -> 13, n=40 -> 29, n=60 -> 46. They are recorded for
completeness only. Because the occupancy gate closes the arm, no forward row
will ever be booked and no kill line is frozen: there is nothing for them to
guard. Stage B does not start for this arm. No revival without a new dated
signed amendment (a revival must fix occupancy and must state barrier units;
see section 5).

## 5. Consequences and queue

1. C-W3 is CLOSED, UNTESTED-LOW-OCCUPANCY, with the negative design gap
   recorded as bookkeeping. Closed arms are never reopened; redefining the
   state, barrier or horizon requires a new dated signed amendment with its
   own Stage 0x/A and its own kill lines.
2. C-W1 (compression-entry hazard, weekly grain, the NQ-style construction on
   BTC weekly bars, pre-registered in the ledger since session 60) is now the
   only open WHEN claim on BTC OHLCV. Its Stage 0x/A needs its own dated
   amendment (Amendment 13 candidate, draft pending; unsigned drafts stay
   unevaluated).
3. The L2 layer is unchanged: blocked on data, C-E1 parity gate first, Bybit
   archive download on Andrea's machine per the frozen ranking.
4. Methodology lesson (to lessons_learned on next upkeep): every barrier
   formula must state its units (log-return vs price-level) at registration;
   a dimensionally incoherent label was caught by the staged gates before it
   could spend anything, which is the gates doing their job.
5. Nothing in this addendum touches: the NQ programme or its spent holdout,
   the macro Amendment 9 arms or their frozen kill lines, the Sunday cadence,
  the V4h nightly task, or any AVFI frozen constant.

Executed and recorded 2026-11-16. Stage 0x/A only; no forward evaluation of
any kind; the 2021+ BTC window remains sealed.

# Amendment 12: The Crypto Accelerator (primed-state hazard on the crypto branch)

Status: SIGNED 2026-11-14. Nothing in the signed amendment is retroactive permission for any computation before the staged gates.

## 0. Provenance corrections, recorded before the claim text

The directive's stated basis contains three sentences that are FALSE PROVENANCE and are corrected here so the claim text starts clean:

1. "The C7 test already proved that coils resolve with a median 8.1% move, and 28.6% of exits contain a >4-sigma day." No C7 concept, test, or statistic exists anywhere in the programme. This is the SECOND occurrence of this sentence pattern; the first ("C7 Primed proven significant") was corrected in the record on 2026-11-12 (session 63): no C7 concept exists, the forward log is empty and is the only proof object. The 8.1% and 28.6% figures exist nowhere in any record, report, or ledger. The state the directive calls "C7 Primed" is the PRIMED DRAFT STATE: Andrea's own v4 dashboard spec (strategies/avfi_research_v4_draft.pine, layer 3), always labeled DRAFT-UNEVALUATED, never registered, never evaluated. This amendment registers it properly; it does not inherit any "proven" status because none exists.
2. "You have proven that the SMH salience gap is a cross-market structural law." Stage A is design bookkeeping on unsealed pre-2021 windows; the frozen rule is design statistics are bookkeeping, never proof. Three positive design gaps raise the prior for the forward test; they prove nothing. The proof object remains the forward calibration record alone, which is empty by design.
3. "The kill lines are the same: frozen, pre-declared, and public." Kill lines are never transferable between arms. The frozen NQ lines (and the GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX lines) are those markets' own; the crypto arm freezes its own lines from its own Stage A design statistics by the frozen rule. What transfers is the DISCIPLINE (frozen, pre-declared, public), not the numbers.

Also recorded: verify_l2_gate.py and c1_l2_test.py do not exist in the pipeline and have never existed. The L2 layer is blocked-on-data per the frozen data audit (crypto_orderflow_claims_v0.md: "Held now: none"): no crypto tick, trade, or L2 data exists in the knowledge folders, and the Bybit archive download must run on Andrea's machine. The primary research object cannot be switched to the BTCUSDT L2 order book by declaration; it becomes a research object when the data exists and the C-E1 engine parity gate has passed.

## 1. The claim (one pre-registration, Phase 1, OHLCV route)

CLAIM: after a primed-state entry on the pre-registered crypto universe (Section 3), the hazard of a 4-sigma barrier touch within 60 calendar days exceeds matched climatology.

Registering as C-W3 in the crypto claims ledger (ledger v1 at signature; C-W1 and C-W2 remain open and untested; family multiplicity with C-W1 declared in Section 6).

STATE (the clock), daily grain, Andrea's primed spec verbatim, untranslated (the v4 dashboard translated it to weekly for the NQ chart; this amendment registers the ORIGINAL daily-grain semantics, matching the directive's comp_days units):
- ATR_d = ATR(30) on daily bars (Wilder).
- rvol_pct = prior-bar percentile rank of ATR_d over the trailing 52 daily bars.
- comp_days = consecutive days with rvol_pct <= 33 (reset to 0 on any day above).
- range_pos = (close - min(low, 365d)) / (max(high, 365d) - min(low, 365d)).
- PRIMED ENTRY = the first day with comp_days >= 20 AND rvol_pct <= 33 AND range_pos <= 0.35, after a day not meeting all three.
- Decision-time computability: every input uses strictly prior data (C-W2 semantics; the determinism and look-ahead audit is part of Stage 0x and is the arm's admission test).

EVENT: within 60 calendar days of the primed entry day, the first daily bar whose HIGH >= entry_close + 4*sigma_d or LOW <= entry_close - 4*sigma_d, where sigma_d = stdev (ddof=1) of the trailing 30 daily log close-to-close returns as of the entry day, and entry_close = the primed entry day's close. Direction REFUSED: both-side touch, whichever side first, never split. Censored (non-event) at day 60 with no event. This is the daily-OHLCV honest form of the directive's "trade-touch through an ATR-scaled barrier"; the L2-measured touch is the Phase 2 refinement (Section 5).

NULL: entry-anchored cum60 hazard equals matched climatology (era-matched unconditional 60-day touch frequencies under the same sigma_d barrier rule, same construction as the macro programme's matched climatology).

FALSIFICATION: kill lines frozen from the arm's OWN Stage A design cum60 by the frozen rule (largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05) at n = 10/20/40/60 forward rows, frozen BEFORE the first forward row, per the house pattern. A breach at any n closes the arm UNTESTED-FAILED-DESIGN. Closed arms are never reopened; changing any frozen constant requires a new dated signed amendment.

## 2. Staging (all gated on signature)

- Stage 0x (design windows only): data feasibility (Yahoo v8 daily for majors; Binance Vision klines for pairs where Yahoo is absent or synthetic-volume, fetch-cap feasibility tested honestly and reported), repair and sanity audit per house rules, primed-state determinism and look-ahead audit, occupancy count. Andrea's action item if he wants Phase 2: run the download. The 4h Realized Range Percentile clock and any 4h aggregation are a Stage 0x feasibility item (Binance Vision klines), not a registered definition; the registered Phase 1 clock is the daily primed state. Priority statement recorded per the directive: the active build is the crypto branch; the macro layer proceeds passively (forward rows at clock entries only).

## 6. Multiplicity, relationships, and non-negotiables

# Amendment 13 candidate (DRAFT, UNSIGNED): the C-W1 BTC weekly compression-entry hazard, Stage 0x/A pre-registration

Status: UNSIGNED DRAFT, zero status, never executed. No computation runs before
Andrea's dated signature, not even Stage 0x. The number "13" is provisional: the
methodology review's D-3 (options term-structure unseen-ness) was also flagged as a
future Amendment 13 candidate; the final number is assigned at signature, and a
collision is resolved by sequence at signing, never by renumbering a signed record.
This draft operationalizes the ONLY open WHEN claim on BTC OHLCV (the ledger v2,
post C-W3 closure): C-W1, registered since session 60, unchanged through ledger
v0/v1/v2. Its Stage 0x/A "needs its own dated amendment" (amendment12_btc_addendum
section 5, item 2); this document is that amendment, awaiting his word.

Citation audit 2026-11-17, session 87: every quoted constant re-checked against
its source (ledger v0/v2, the C-W3 addendum, smh_hazard_v0, the cross-market
Stage A, the Amendment 9 market addendum, stage0x_stageA.py's 18 parity checks,
the robustness audit, the mid/stress clocks). All numbers PASS. One wording
defect corrected on the record below: the family-register decline line was
asserted as already recorded; it is now stated as a signature-time action,
because unsigned drafts give the register no status. No number moved.

## 0. What this draft is and is not

It is a pre-registration: the full definition, staged gates, kill lines and forward
protocol, fixed BEFORE any computation, so that nothing can be tuned after seeing a
number. It is not evidence, not a test, not a trade, and it licenses no claim about
any market. Design statistics computed later at Stage A are bookkeeping, never
proof; the proof object for this claim, as for every SMH clock, is the forward
calibration record alone.

## 1. The claim, verbatim, and its operationalization decision

C-W1 (ledger v0, carried unchanged): "after a pre-registered compression-range
entry on BTC (daily and weekly, genuine-volume data), the hazard of a
volatility-scaled significant move within 12 bars exceeds matched climatology."
Mechanism: volatility persistence and mean reversion (GARCH-type clustering);
compression precedes expansion; duration dependence of the release. Test:
entry-anchored hazard (KM) vs matched climatology. Kill line: entry cum-hazard at
12 bars fails to clear the climatology band under the frozen definition; forward
kill lines at pre-declared row counts if promoted (house pattern 3/8/19/31 as the
NQ reference values).

The "daily and weekly" language is operationalized HERE, before any data decision:
the WEEKLY grain is the claim's primary and only arm. The NQ-style construction
(sig_w, weekly bars, 12-week horizon) is intrinsically weekly; a daily-grain
analogue has no frozen definition, and the C-W3 experience is the warning: the
stricter daily compression clock on BTC produced 7 entries in 6.3 years and died
at the occupancy gate. The daily variant is PRE-DECLINED in this draft, and enters
the family register as declined-before-test AT SIGNATURE, with the signature
date as the decline's date (declining before the test is honest; declining after
results is not; until signature the register does not carry the decline, because
unsigned drafts have no status). Running it later requires a new dated signed
amendment with its own definition, and it joins the same family under max-t.

## 2. The construction, frozen (NQ parity, transplanted by rule, never tuned)

Everything below is the frozen NQ SMH construction (smh_hazard_v0 definitions,
stage0c conventions) transposed to BTC weekly bars by fixed rule. No alternative
value, window, boundary or threshold is computed or compared at any stage. This is
the no-forking clause; any deviation discovered mid-flight closes the stage and
returns here for a dated amendment.

- Data: the audited BTC daily canonical (data/btc_daily_canonical.csv, Yahoo
  BTC-USD, 2014-09-17 to present, Stage 0x-audited in the C-W3 addendum: 4444 raw
  rows, zero repairs, zero gaps). Price columns only.
- Genuine-volume clause: the construction below uses NO volume feature anywhere;
  the claim's "genuine-volume data" requirement is therefore satisfied by
  construction, and the C-W3 consolidated-volume caveat is void here. If any volume
  feature is ever added (an AVFI-style layer would need genuine exchange volume),
  that is a new dated amendment; consolidated-feed volume is never a feature.
- Weekly bars: UTC ISO weeks, Monday 00:00 UTC boundary, close = the last daily
  close of the week. BTC trades 24/7, so no calendar repair policy is needed; the
  forming week never enters any computation.
- sig_w(t): 8-week trailing stdev (ddof=1) of weekly log close-to-close returns,
  on completed weeks only.
- The compression cut, by the frozen Amendment 9 rank-transfer convention:
  LOW_BTC is the percentile rank of NQ's frozen LOW (0.01739, the 27.52nd
  percentile of NQ's pre-2021 sig_w distribution) applied to BTC's OWN pre-2021
  (unsealed) design-window sig_w distribution. The macro Stage A used exactly this
  convention (GOLD 0.016023, NIKKEI 0.019622, DAX 0.019349); the NQ absolute cuts
  are context only and are never used. LOW_BTC is computed once at Stage 0x,
  recorded, and never re-tuned.
- Compression entry at week t (a cross, not a dwell): sig_w(t) <= LOW_BTC AND
  sig_w(t-1) > LOW_BTC.
- Significant 1-week event at decision week t (event in week t+1):
  |r1(t+1)| >= 2.5 x sig_w(t). The threshold updates weekly with the decision
  week's own vol; it is a ratio of a return to a return stdev, dimensionally
  coherent by construction (the C-W3 barrier lesson is structural here, not a
  repair that can recur).
- First event: the earliest h in 1..12 with no earlier flagged week since entry;
  KM person-week construction; episodes with NaN or sample end inside the window
  are censored per KM convention.
- Direction REFUSED: the event is the absolute value, both-side, never split. The
  NQ direction peeks are NQ-registered only; BTC v0 carries NO direction peeks.
  Adding any is a new dated amendment.

## 3. Stage 0x (admission tests, before any design number)

1. Data re-audit of the daily canonical (nulls, nonpositives, high<low,
   duplicates, gaps) and a weekly aggregation audit (week count, boundary check,
   no double-counted days).
2. LOW_BTC computed by the frozen rank-transfer rule and recorded.
3. Determinism: the full Stage 0x/A report reproduced byte-identically on a
   second run.
4. Look-ahead audit: every design entry's state recomputed from data truncated at
   the entry week, exact match required (the C-W2 semantics that served as the
   C-W3 admission test).
5. OCCUPANCY GATE: at least 10 true compression entries in the pre-2021 design
   window. Below 10, the arm CLOSES UNTESTED-LOW-OCCUPANCY (H7 and C-W3
   precedent) and nothing downstream runs. Honest prior: at the macro markets'
   cross rates (roughly 2 entries per year across GOLD/NIKKEI/DAX and NQ) a 6.3
   year design window implies roughly 11 to 15 entries; BTC's vol regime is
   different from every market tested so far, and this gate genuinely may fail.
   That is the gate doing its job.

The 2021+ window is SEALED: counted for availability only, never computed on,
never described, matching the macro-market and C-W3 discipline. It is a fresh
sealed holdout, openable at most once, only on Andrea's dated go, under the
standing hard rule; this draft registers no computation on it.

## 4. Stage A (design bookkeeping, pre-2021 only, descriptive, never proof)

Using the signed Amendment 9 Stage A machinery (research/stage0x_stageA.py, the
independent reimplementation with its 18-check NQ parity gate, already green
first-run on the macro markets), parameterized for BTC weekly bars:

- Entry-anchored 12-week first-event KM: cum12 with Greenwood 95 percent band,
  median lag, the hazard shape.
- Matched climatology: BTC's own all-decision-weeks 12-week first-event cum-hazard
  with its Greenwood band (the C0 convention, smh_robust climatology semantics),
  era-banded (2-year blocks, descriptive dispersion reported alongside, D-4
  convention as a reporting note).
- THE GAP (the one quantity the NQ definition audits established as robust, and
  the quantity that replicated positive in all three macro markets: +0.136, +0.188,
  +0.215 against NQ's +0.193): entry cum12 minus own climatology cum12.
- DESIGN-STAGE KILL: if the design cum12 fails to clear the matched climatology
  band (the bands do not separate), the arm CLOSES UNTESTED-FAILED-DESIGN before
  any forward row. The C-W3 arm closed one gate earlier with a negative gap
  (-0.0572); the same honesty applies here.

## 5. Kill lines and the forward protocol (only if Stage A survives)

1. Forward kill lines are frozen from the arm's OWN design cum12 by the frozen
   binomial rule (largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05 at n = 10/20/40/60
   scored rows), recorded at Stage A end, BEFORE the first forward row. The macro
   precedent: NQ 3/8/19/31, GOLD 3/8/19/30, NIKKEI 2/7/16/26, DAX 3/9/21/34, each
   from its own design cum12. BTC's values are computed at Stage A and recorded
   here by appendix when the stage runs; they do not exist before then.
2. Forward rows accrue from SIGNATURE ONLY: BTC clock entries beginning after the
   amendment is signed, booked at the Sunday cadence (the macro booking pattern,
   book_macro_rows.py style, per-market cuts and log), append-only, direction
   blank, scored against the frozen kill lines from the first row. No backfill
   from the sealed window, ever.
3. Booking mechanism honesty (the V4h lesson): before the first reliance on any
   scheduled booking, the mechanism's operation is verified live in this
   workspace's task systems; if the task is not visible, the booking runs manually
   at the Sunday cadence and the record says so. The chain is never silently
   trusted.

## 7. Power readout BEFORE sign-off (what a null can and cannot tell)

- The design window (2014-09-17 to 2020-12-31, about 330 weekly bars) is short;
  Greenwood bands at 10-15 entries are wide. A Stage A gap inside wide bands is
  uninformative in BOTH directions: it can neither promote nor quietly kill.
  The design-stage kill fires only on band failure, not on weakness.
- Forward arithmetic, honestly conditional: IF BTC's cross rate resembles the
  tested markets (roughly 2 entries per year), the first kill-line gate at 10
  scored rows arrives about 5 years after signature, the second about 10. If the
  rate is lower (the C-W3 warning), longer. This is the same measured honesty as
  the methodology review's time-to-proof arithmetic; no accelerant is claimed for
  BTC beyond its own rows.
- A null result (a kill at any gate) tells: the transplanted construction does
  not carry entry-conditioned hazard structure on BTC OHLCV at this grain. It
  cannot tell: whether a different (unfrozen) definition would, whether L2
  microstructure carries one (that is C1-L2, a different claim family), anything
  about direction, or anything about any other asset.
- A pass at every gate tells: one more clock, on one more asset, with the same
  actuarial status as the others. It is never a trade recommendation, and
  post-cost exploitability remains expressly outside the claim.

## 8. Book-layer grounding (the skill's standing requirement)

Volatility persistence and mean reversion: the econometrics layer (Tsay, the
GARCH family) and the NQ record's own measured salience gradient (0.634 entry,
0.470 mid, 0.441 climatology, 0.273 stress-side suppression). Duration
dependence: the survival spine (Kaplan-Meier, Greenwood), the clinical bridge the
programme has used since the NQ design. Transfer evidence: the signed Amendment 9
Stage A, where the same construction transferred by rank replicated the gap sign
in three markets with different regimes, currencies and calendars, zero re-tuning.
Validation spine: family max-t, persistence-preserving nulls (Lessons 11/13), no
backtest as evidence, the forward calibration record as the only proof object.

## 9. What this draft cannot tell

Direction, causality, microstructure mechanism (that is the L2 layer's question),
post-cost exploitability, anything about any pair or market other than BTC at
weekly grain, and anything at all until the stages run and the rows accrue.

# Amendment 14 candidate (DRAFT, UNSIGNED): the options term-structure unseen-ness pre-registration (methodology-review candidate D-3)

Status: UNSIGNED DRAFT, zero status, never executed. No computation runs before
Andrea's dated signature, not even a data audit. The number "14" is provisional:
the methodology review flagged this candidate (D-3) as a future "Amendment 13",
and the C-W1 pre-registration draft currently holds the provisional number 13;
the final number is assigned at signature in signing order, and a collision is
resolved by sequence, never by renumbering a signed record. This draft exists
because the review's own discipline demands the definition BEFORE the data
decision: "define the readout and its null BEFORE any data decision, with a power
analysis from the VXN analogue" (smh_methodology_review_v0, section 5, D-3).
The data source is Andrea's choice at signature, listed in section 4; nothing
below depends on which source he picks.

## 0. What this draft is and is not

It is a pre-registration of ONE descriptive readout: the last unmeasured cell of
the unseen-ness layer (smh_unseen_v0 section 1c, the event-timing structure). It
is not a clock, not a WHEN claim, not a trade, and it changes no frozen SMH
constant, no kill line, no forward protocol, no registered claim. Whatever it
finds, the proof object of every SMH clock remains the forward calibration
record alone; a level is not a dated hazard (smh_unseen_v0 section 2), so no
verdict here can promote, extend or shorten any clock's schedule.

## 1. The question and the measured boundary, from the record

The unseen-ness layer measured three answers on the pre-2021 design sample and
left one boundary unmeasured. Already measured, NOT re-asked here:

- LEVEL: compression entries sit at VXN's 25.8th percentile (median 16.47 vs
  20.14 all weeks; region occupancy percentiles 22.5 / 42.4 / 77.7): the entry
  is level-cheap and implied vol tracks the clock monotonically.
- MEAN: the variance risk premium survives the trigger (+3.71 vol points at
  entries vs +4.55 all weeks; positive at 73.2% of entries vs 76.7% of weeks):
  the strong "cheap optionality" claim is REFUTED at the mean level in the
  design sample.
- The unmeasured remainder, verbatim (smh_unseen_v0 section 1c): "whether the
  specific first-significant-week hazard (0.634 within 12 weeks, median lag 5,
  the 3.5-sigma median event) is priced cannot be read from weekly index closes:
  that would need the options term structure (is the VXN curve flat/cheap at
  compression entries?) or option prices themselves (the implied probability of
  a >=2.5 sigma week within 12 weeks). Out of scope for weekly data; recorded,
  not actioned."

This draft actiones that standing need (first registered in smh_hazard_v0
section 6) as a pre-registered readout, nothing more.

## 2. The readout, frozen before any data

PRIMARY QUANTITY (the headline): the options market's own implied probability of
the SMH event, at compression entries, compared with the SMH design number.
From the chosen source, at each decision week: the implied probability that
the NDX-linked underlying moves at least 2.5 x sigma_w(t) within one week at
some point in the next 12 weeks, extracted from option prices at the Friday
close of the decision week t by the source's own model-free convention
(Breeden-Litzenberger state-price density if the source gives a full chain;
the source's quoted 12-week implied move cone if it does not). sigma_w(t) is
the frozen SMH yardstick at the decision week (8-week trailing, ddof=1, weekly
log returns); the event definition is the frozen SMH event, transplanted
unchanged. THE GAP: the median implied event probability at the 41 pre-2021
compression entries minus the SMH design cum12, 0.634 [Greenwood 0.487, 0.782].
Matched climatology: the same implied probability at all pre-2021 decision
weeks with a full 12-week forward window (the C0 convention), median and
Greenwood-style bootstrap band (B = 2000, block = 13 weeks, seed = 42, the
house bootstrap constants), era-banded (2-year blocks, descriptive dispersion
alongside the pooled point: the D-4 convention as a reporting note).

SECONDARY QUANTITY (recorded, never headline): the term-structure SLOPE at
entries vs matched weeks (implied vol at the nearest maturity quote on or
beyond 12 weeks minus the nearest liquid maturity), in the source's own quoted
units, the same comparison structure. It is the section-1c question in its
cheapest form and it is reported in the same table; it exists so that a source
which cannot price the full event still answers the curve-shape question.

PRE-DECLARED VERDICTS, exactly three, all descriptive:

1. PRICED: the entry implied-probability band overlaps the SMH design band
   [0.487, 0.782]. The event structure is priced; unseen-ness fails at the
   event level too, and the observer corollary rests entirely on the
   calibration record. Recorded, no trading implication.
2. UNDERPRICED: the entry band lies wholly below 0.487. The crowd prices less
   event than the design clock measured. Recorded as a descriptive fact about
   the design sample; it licenses no trade, no rule change, and no claim that
   the pricing error persists forward.
3. OVERPRICED: the entry band lies wholly above 0.782. The crowd prices MORE
   event than the design clock measured; consistent with the surviving variance
   risk premium at a finer grain. Same descriptive status.

Bands overlapping in one direction only (an edge inside the other band) is a
NULL readout, recorded as null with both bands printed. The readout is
two-sided by construction: no direction, no asymmetry, no second comparison
may be added after seeing the numbers.

DESIGN-STAGE ADMISSION TESTS (before any readout number):

1. Source provenance enters docs/provenance_manifest.md first (fetch dates,
   coverage, licensing note; never a licensed dataset into the repo).
2. Coverage audit: term-structure or option quotes located for the entry dates.
   COVERAGE GATE: at least 33 of the 41 compression entries (80 percent) must
   carry a usable quote at their decision Friday, else the readout CLOSES
   UNTESTED-POOR-COVERAGE (the C-W3 occupancy precedent applied to a
   descriptive readout). The threshold is an admission gate fixed before any
   data was seen, not a tuned parameter; any other pre-declared threshold
   would be equally arbitrary, and this one is now frozen.
3. Determinism: the full report reproduced byte-identically on a second run.
4. Look-ahead audit: every entry's implied probability recomputed from source
   data truncated at the entry Friday, exact match required (the C-W2
   semantics).
5. No 2021+ data enters anything: the readout is pre-2021 design bookkeeping;
   the 2021+ options data remains sealed with the rest of the holdout.

## 3. Power readout BEFORE sign-off (the VXN analogue)

n = 41 entries against a matched pool of roughly a thousand decision weeks. The
VXN analogue is the honest calibration of what this n can see: the level
medians (16.47 vs 20.14) separated clearly because the effect was large; the
premium medians (+3.71 vs +4.55) sat inside episode noise and were reported as
medians only, no test. An entry-vs-climatology difference in implied event
probability smaller than roughly 5 points is likely unreadable at n = 41; only
a large mispricing is detectable. A null (bands overlapping) is therefore
uninformative in BOTH directions: it can neither confirm unseen-ness nor
refute it. This ceiling is fixed by the design sample and does not move with
any choice below. The decision this draft asks of Andrea is whether the
question is worth asking at this power ceiling at all; the draft itself takes
no position.

## 4. Data-source candidates (his choice at signature; the definitions above do not change with the source)

(a) NDX options chains via CBOE DataShop (direct route to the primary
    quantity; paid, his hands, provenance-manifested).
(b) VXN/VX futures term structure, EOD (secondary-quantity route only;
    cheaper coverage, coarser answer; if this route is chosen the primary
    quantity is NOT computed and the readout proceeds on the slope alone,
    stated plainly in the report).
(c) His broker's options export (depends on coverage of the 2001-2020 entry
    dates; the coverage gate decides, not preference).
Pre-declined: intraday options data (out of scope for a weekly-grain
question); any synthetic or interpolated term structure built by this
programme (the readout reads the crowd's prices; it must not read our own
model's); VIX spot as a substitute (already measured, section 1 above).

## 5. Multiplicity, family, and status

This readout joins no WHEN family (it is not a hazard claim), opens no clock,
and adds no forward row to any log. It is one pre-registered comparison, one
cell, no grid: no alternative window, maturity band, or probability
convention may be computed alongside it, and whatever looks interesting after
the fact stays descriptive, flagged, and never trusted until separately
pre-registered (the seductive-cell register discipline). Its verdict, if any,
is a fact about the design sample's pricing, recorded in the unseen-ness
layer as the closure (or the last measured boundary) of the question
smh_unseen_v0 opened. Closed verdicts stand.

## 6. What this draft cannot tell

Whether any pricing gap persists forward (design bookkeeping, pre-2021 only);
whether the gap is exploitable after costs (expressly outside); direction;
anything about microstructure (that is the L2 layer's question); anything
about any market other than NDX-linked options; and anything at all until the
source is chosen, the gates run, and the numbers append to this document.

## 7. Signature block

Unsigned. On Andrea's dated signature WITH a data-source choice recorded,
this becomes a signed amendment; the provenance entry, admission-test results
and readout append as numbered sections, never as rewrites. Until then,
nothing in this file has status, the standing data need remains documented
and not actioned, and no tool may act on it.

Refused by Andrea at the directorate sitting held in the main conversation.
His clarified answer: refuse this draft, and author a BTC-options

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/amendment15_till_l2_top50_flow_panel.md (5351 bytes)
# Amendment 15 (ARMED, 2026-11-20): the Till-L2 Top-50 Flow-Proxy Panel

Status: ARMED by the Board's dated word, recorded verbatim below. This is the
interim study the Board ordered for the period until L2 data arrives. It is
written entirely BEFORE any market data is touched. Plain English is used on
purpose, at the Board's request.

## 0. What this is, plainly

A small forward-only test. Each day, on each of 50 coins, the VPVI flow
machinery prints a rare "exhaustion" signal. The question is one: after a
signal, does price move the opposite way over the next 20 daily bars more
often than after random bars? Signals dated after 2026-11-20 are scored as
they arrive. Nothing older is ever scored. The study dies the day the TARDIS
API key lands. Nobody should expect it to find anything: the power readout
in section 7 says it cannot, in the time it has. Its honest product is a
clean forward log, not a discovery.

## 1. The authorizing word

The Board was offered a top-50 scan in session 146 and refused it under the
gates (the signed A12 Option A BTC-only freeze, the dated-amendment rule,
and the hindsight problem with reading finished charts). Two lawful doors
were offered. The Board answered, 2026-11-20, in the main conversation:
"Just do it till l2 please". That word is the signature. By it, the A12
Option A crypto universe freeze is lifted for THIS study only. Everything
else the freeze covers stays frozen. The NQ archive is untouched. The L2
track stays the only real front.

## 2. The coins (the universe)

The first executing session fetches the 50 largest coins by market value from
one named public source (CoinGecko), records the list, the date, and the web
address in the record, and freezes it. Delisted coins stay on the list as
dead rows. Never re-ranked. Never replaced.

## 3. The data, and what may be done with it

Daily bars, 24/7 crypto feed, from the first source that works in this
order: (a) a data connection the Board turns on (BigQuery), (b) the Board's
own export, (c) scheduled public fetches if the environment allows. Start-up
needs only the trailing 300 days per coin to initialize the indicators.
Those bars are input-only. The ONLY bars ever scored are signal bars dated
after 2026-11-20. No backtest. No reading of old history. The sealed 2021+
crypto windows stay sealed against backward study; forward rows are, and
have always been, the lawful use.

## 4. The two arms

Arm A1, the corrected formula. The programme's own fix, made before the
first run ever happens (design, not re-tuning: nothing has ever run on
market data): the bar's close position drives the delta,
delta = volume * (2*close - high - low) / (high - low),
so a bar that spikes up and dies on its lows now correctly reads as
selling. The acceptance layer (150-bar channel break on the cumulative,
held 5 bars) and the capped-z shock dots stay exactly as pasted.

Arm A2, the pasted formula, frozen as reviewed. Deliberately kept, defect
included (it never sees the close), as the comparator. Its job is to measure
what the defect costs. Not an endorsement of the third-party lineage.

Two arms. No third arm. No variants. No re-tuning ever.

## 5. What gets scored

An exhaustion signal up (the flow breaks to a new high and holds) counts as
a top call: the next 20 daily bars are watched. A signal down counts as a
bottom call, same window. Each real signal is compared against 10 random
bars from the same coin in the same volatility regime. Win = the 20-bar
return after the signal goes the opposite way of the flow, beaten by the
honest margin of the matched controls, pooled across all 50 coins, one arm
at a time.

## 6. The statistics, kill lines, and the sunset

Two primary arms only. One-sided family-wise 5 percent, split evenly
(Sidak, 0.0253 each) with the block bootstrap by coin. Per-coin readouts
are forbidden forever (no coin can reach a lawful sample before the study
dies). At 30 pooled scored signals per arm, if the arm has not cleared its
family test, the arm is DEAD in the record and is never re-armed except by
the Board's new dated word with new frozen terms. Interim counts at 10
signals are descriptive only, zero status. SUNSET: the day TARDIS_API_KEY
lands and the C1-L2 work begins, the panel closes, whatever rows exist
stand, and the expected outcome is an informative nothing. If the math says
dead, the obituary is written.

## 7. The power readout (computed before sign-off, no market data)

Per-arm one-sided alpha 0.0253, 80 percent power needs:
- n=10 signals: effect 0.88 sigma; n=30: 0.51; n=50: 0.40; n=100: 0.28.
- A small true effect (0.2 sigma) needs 196 signals (years).
- The panel expects 6 to 30 signals total before the sunset.
- Verdict: nothing plausible in this family's measured record (H8
  wrong-signed at n=32, H9 n=3 in 26 years, both null and closed) can be
  detected in the window this study has. The Board signed with this number
  in view. The default stands: NO EDGE, until the forward record says
  otherwise.

## 8. What stays forbidden

No trades. No backtest as evidence. No label changes. No kill-line moves.
No per-coin stories. No subgroup digging. No 100-150 quick tests across
coins (the multiplicity machine the gates block). No endorsement of the
VPVI lineage. The two load-bearing rules carry: no edge by default, and the
burden of proof absolute.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/amendment15_till_l2_top50_flow_panel.md

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/docs/amendment2_U3_sizing.md (2606 bytes)
# Amendment 2 (proposed, pending Andrea's sign-off): rule set U3 sizing overlay

Proposed 11 Nov 2026 after stage 4c. Not in force until Andrea approves.

## What is proposed

Add one candidate to the single-open holdout battery: rule set U3, defined
exactly as tested in stage 4c:

- Signal: the registered rule set T signal, unchanged (12-week log return of
  the weekly close positive at the prior close). Lookback stays frozen at 12.
- Position size (decided at the same prior close, fractional):
  w = T_signal * min(1, 0.20 / vol8) * (0.5 if AVFI state = reversal_down
  else 1.0), where vol8 = annualized stdev of the last 8 weekly log returns.
- Constants pre-committed before evaluation: vol window 8, target 20%
  annualized, throttle 0.5, cap 1.0. No scans were run.
- Costs: 0.3625 pts per side per unit of exposure change.
- Holdout convention (for battery parity with T): the registered next-open
  fill engine applies; the fractional size applies to the position held, with
  size changes executed at next open and charged pro rata.

## Unsealed evidence (2007-09 to 2020-12, 693 weeks)

| metric | T (binary) | U3 |
|---|---|---|
| annualized Sharpe | 0.626 | 0.639 |
| max drawdown | -21.5% | -14.2% |
| total return | +217% | +148% |
| leave-one-year-out Sharpe min | 0.505 | 0.516 |
| excl. 2008-09 Sharpe | 0.565 | 0.560 |
| bootstrap p (Bonnferroni x3) | - | 0.015 |

Honest reading: U3's Sharpe edge is small and its drawdown edge is
crisis-driven (excluding 2008-09 it matches T). It is defensive insurance
paid for with upside. Its reversal_down throttle gives the AVFI indicator a
measurable job (a risk throttle, not an entry timer). It was developed after
seeing T's characteristics on the same window, so in-sample selection risk is
real and only the holdout or forward paper testing can settle it.

## Battery change if approved

- Family becomes two tested hypotheses (T and U3), max-t adjusted across
  both; the H7 gate and its tests remain unchanged (and will remain
  untestable on current occupancy).
- U3 passes the battery at demeaned block-bootstrap p < 0.05 (B=2000,
  block=13, seed=42) after max-t adjustment.
- A stage 6b variant script (stage6b_v2_amendment2.py) implements this test
  and remains hard-gated behind the same go argument. The original stage 6b
  script is untouched; which battery runs depends entirely on Andrea's
  decision on this amendment.

## If not approved

U3 remains a development candidate, documented in stage 4c, and can be tested
only through forward paper trading alongside T, with both logged separately
in the stage 7 Sunday log.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/docs/amendment2_U3_sizing.md
